{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nThis is the environment state manager for the LLM agent.\\nauthor: Pingyue Zhang\\ndate: 2025-03-30\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nimport atexit\\nfrom concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor\\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\\nfrom typing import Dict, List, Optional, Any, Union\\nimport PIL.Image\\nimport hydra\\nimport random\\nimport numpy as np\\nimport logging\\n\\nfrom ragen.env import REGISTERED_ENVS, REGISTERED_ENV_CONFIGS\\nfrom ragen.utils import register_resolvers\\nregister_resolvers()\\n\\n@dataclass\\nclass EnvStatus:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Status of an environment\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    truncated: bool = False # done but not success\\n    terminated: bool = False # done and success\\n    num_actions: int = 0 # current action step (single action)\\n    rewards: List[float] = field(default_factory=list) # rewards for each turn\\n    seed: Optional[int] = None # what seed is used to reset this environment\\n\\n\\n\\nclass EnvStateManager:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Manager for the environment state\\n    The class is responsible for managing multiple (kinds of) environments\\n    \\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    def __init__(self, config, mode: str = \\\"train\\\"):\\n        self.sys_config = config\\n        self.mode = mode\\n        self.config = getattr(self.sys_config.es_manager, mode)\\n        self.env_groups = int(self.config.env_groups)\\n        self.group_size = self.config.group_size\\n        seed_cfg = getattr(self.sys_config, \\\"seed\\\", None)\\n        if seed_cfg is not None:\\n            self.base_seed = seed_cfg.get(mode, None)\\n        else:\\n            self.base_seed = None\\n        self.seed_counter = 0\\n        self._init_envs()\\n        self.rollout_cache = None\\n        self._executors: Dict[str, ThreadPoolExecutor] = {}\\n        self._executors_shutdown = False\\n        self._register_parallel_executors()\\n        atexit.register(self._shutdown_executors)\\n\\n    def _init_envs(self):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Initialize the environments. train_envs and val_envs are lists of envs:\\n        Input: tags: [\\\"SimpleSokoban\\\", \\\"HarderSokoban\\\"]; n_groups: [1, 1]; group_size: 16\\n        Output: envs: List[Dict], each **entry** is a dict with keys: tag, group_id, env_id, env, env_config, status\\n        Example: [{\\\"tag\\\": \\\"SimpleSokoban\\\", \\\"group_id\\\": 0, \\\"env_id\\\": 0, \\\"env\\\": env, \\\"config\\\": env_config, \\\"status\\\": EnvStatus()},\\n            ...\\n            {\\\"tag\\\": \\\"SimpleSokoban\\\", \\\"group_id\\\": 0, \\\"env_id\\\": 15 (group_size - 1), ...},\\n            {\\\"tag\\\": \\\"HarderSokoban\\\", \\\"group_id\\\": 1, \\\"env_id\\\": 16, ...}\\n            ...]\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        assert sum(self.config.env_configs.n_groups) == self.env_groups, f\\\"Sum of n_groups must equal env_groups. Include admissible actions in render cache\\n            self.render_cache = self._format_observation(obs[0], self.available_actions)\\n            return self.render_cache\\n\\n        except (RuntimeError, RuntimeWarning) as e:\\n            print(f\\\"Error in reset: {e}\\\")\\n            next_seed = abs(hash(str(seed))) % (2 ** 32) if seed is not None else None\\n            return self.reset(next_seed, mode=self.current_mode)\\n\\n    def _format_observation(self, obs: str, admissible_actions: list) -> str:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Format observation to include admissible actions.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        actions_str = \\\", \\\".join(admissible_actions)\\n        return f\\\"{obs}\\\\n\\\\nAdmissible actions: [{actions_str}]\\\"\\n    \\n    def step(self, action: str):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        Take a step in the environment using the provided action string.\\n\\n        Reward scheme (matching verl-agent):\\n        - reward = config.score * won (10.0 on success, 0.0 otherwise)\\n\\n        Args:\\n            action: The action string to execute\\n\\n        Returns:\\n            observation: Updated observation with admissible actions\\n            reward: Sparse reward (score on success, 0 otherwise)\\n            done: Whether episode is finished\\n            info: Additional information dict\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        action_is_available = action in self.available_actions\\n\\n        # Mode mapping: RAGEN mode -> AlfWorld train_eval\\n    MODE_MAP = {\\n        'train': 'train',\\n        'val': None,  # Will use config.eval_dataset\\n        'test': None,  # Will use config.eval_dataset\\n    }\\n\\n    def __init__(self, config: AlfredEnvConfig = AlfredEnvConfig(), mode='train'):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        Initialize AlfWorld environment.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            config: AlfredEnvConfig instance\\n            mode: 'train' or 'val' - determines which dataset split to use\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        super().__init__()\\n        self.config = config\\n        self.raw_env_config = load_config(self.config.config_file)\\n\\n        # Map RAGEN mode to AlfWorld train_eval\\n        self.current_mode = mode\\n        self._alfworld_mode = self._get_alfworld_mode(mode)\\n\\n        # Initialize raw environment\\n        self.raw_env = self._get_cached_raw_env(self._alfworld_mode)\\n        self.num_games = self.raw_env.num_games\\n        self.game_files = list(self.raw_env.game_files)\\n\\n        self.alfred_env = None\\n        self.current_game_file = None\\n        self.render_cache = None\\n        self.available_actions = None\\n        self.render_mode = self.config.render_mode\\n        assert self.render_mode == 'text'\\n\\n    def _get_alfworld_mode(self, mode: str) -> str:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Convert RAGEN mode to AlfWorld train_eval string.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        if mode == 'train':\\n            return 'train'\\n        else:\\n            # Use eval_dataset from config for val/test modes\\n            return self.config.eval_dataset\\n\\n    def _get_cached_raw_env(self, alfworld_mode: str) -> AlfredTWEnv:\\n        cache_key = (os.path.abspath(self.config.config_file), alfworld_mode)\\n        raw_env = _RAW_ENV_CACHE.get(cache_key)\\n        if raw_env is None:\\n            raw_env = AlfredTWEnv(config=self.raw_env_config, train_eval=alfworld_mode)\\n            _RAW_ENV_CACHE[cache_key] = raw_env\\n        return raw_env\\n    \\n    def _reinitialize_for_mode(self, mode: str):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Reinitialize the environment when mode changes.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        new_alfworld_mode = self._get_alfworld_mode(mode)\\n\\n        if new_alfworld_mode != self._alfworld_mode:\\n            # Close existing environment\\n            if hasattr(self, 'alfred_env') and self.alfred_env is not None:\\n                try:\\n                    self.alfred_env.close()\\n                except Exception:\\n                    pass\\n\\n            # Reinitialize with new mode\\n            self._alfworld_mode = new_alfworld_mode\\n            self.current_mode = mode\\n            self.raw_env = self._get_cached_raw_env(self._alfworld_mode)\\n            self.num_games = self.raw_env.num_games\\n            self.game_files = list(self.raw_env.game_files)\\n            self.alfred_env = None\\n\\n    def reset(self, seed=None, mode=None):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        Reset the environment with a specific seed.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            seed: Random seed for game selection\\n            mode: 'train', 'val', or 'test' - if different from current mode,\\n                  reinitializes the environment with the new dataset split\\n\\n        Returns:\\n            observation: Initial observation text with admissible actions\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        try:\\n            # Handle mode switching dynamically\\n            if mode is not None and mode != self.current_mode:\\n                self._reinitialize_for_mode(mode)\\n            elif mode is not None:\\n\\n\\n... [截断: 输出过长，省略 1086 字符] ...\\n\\nx_steps = self.raw_env_config[\\\"rl\\\"][\\\"training\\\"][\\\"max_nb_steps_per_episode\\\"]\\n\\n            env_id = textworld.gym.register_game(\\n                selected_game,\\n                request_infos=request_infos,\\n                batch_size=1,\\n                asynchronous=False,\\n                max_episode_steps=max_steps,\\n                wrappers=wrappers\\n            )\\n\\n            self.alfred_env = textworld.gym.make(env_id)\\n\\n            obs, info = self.alfred_env.reset()\\n            self.available_actions = info[\\\"admissible_commands\\\"][0]\\n            self.instruction_text = obs[0]\\n            # Include admissible actions in render cache\\n            self.render_cache = self._format_observation(obs[0], self.available_actions)\\n            return self.render_cache\\n\\n        except (RuntimeError, RuntimeWarning) as e:\\n            print(f\\\"Error in reset: {e}\\\")\\n            next_seed = abs(hash(str(seed))) % (2 ** 32) if seed is not None else None\\n            return self.reset(next_seed, mode=self.current_mode)\\n\\n    def _format_observation(self, obs: str, admissible_actions: list) -> str:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Format observation to include admissible actions.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        actions_str = \\\", \\\".join(admissible_actions)\\n        return f\\\"{obs}\\\\n\\\\nAdmissible actions: [{actions_str}]\\\"\\n    \\n    def step(self, action: str):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        Take a step in the environment using the provided action string.\\n\\n        Reward scheme (matching verl-agent):\\n        - reward = config.score * won (10.0 on success, 0.0 otherwise)\\n\\n        Args:\\n            action: The action string to execute\\n\\n        Returns:\\n            observation: Updated observation with admissible actions\\n            reward: Sparse reward (score on success, 0 otherwise)\\n            done: Whether episode is finished\\n            info: Additional information dict\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        action_is_available = action in self.available_actions\\n\\n        # Execute action in environment\\n        obs, _, dones, infos = self.alfred_env.step([action])\\n        observation = obs[0]\\n        self.available_actions = infos[\\\"admissible_commands\\\"][0]\\n        done = dones[0]\\n        won = infos[\\\"won\\\"][0]\\n\\n        # Simplified reward matching verl-agent: score * won\\n        reward = self.config.score * float(won)\\n\\n        # Format observation with admissible actions\\n        self.render_cache = self._format_observation(observation, self.available_actions)\\n\\n        info = {\\n            \\\"action_is_effective\\\": True,\\n            \\\"action_is_valid\\\": action_is_available,\\n            \\\"success\\\": won\\n        }\\n\\n        return self.render_cache, reward, done, info\\n    \\n    def render(self):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Return current observation with admissible actions.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        return self.render_cache\\n\\n    def close(self):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Clean up environment resources.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        self.render_cache = None\\n        if hasattr(self, 'alfred_env') and self.alfred_env is not None:\\n            try:\\n                self.alfred_env.close()\\n            except Exception:\\n                pass\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    import os\\n    os.environ[\\\"ALFWORLD_DATA\\\"] = os.path.expanduser(\\\"~/.cache/alfworld\\\")\\n\\n    # Test basic environment functionality\\n    print(\\\"\\\\n=== Test 1: Basic environment with train mode ===\\\")\\n    env = AlfredTXTEnv()\\n    obs = env.reset(seed=42, mode='train')\\n    print(f\\\"Train observation (first 500 chars):\\\\n{obs[:500]}...\\\")\\n\\n    # Test mode switching\\n    print(\\\"\\\\n=== Test 2: Mode switching to val ===\\\")\\n    obs = env.reset(seed=42, mode='val')\\n    print(f\\\"Val observation (first 500 chars):\\\\n{obs[:500]}...\\\")\\n\\n    # Test step with admissible action\\n    print(\\\"\\\\n=== Test 3: Taking a step ===\\\")\\n    if env.available_actions:\\n        action = env.available_actions[0]\\n        print(f\\\"Taking action: {action}\\\")\\n        obs, reward, done, info = env.step(action)\\n        print(f\\\"Reward: {reward}, Done: {done}, Info: {info}\\\")\\n        print(f\\\"New observation (first 300 chars):\\\\n{obs[:300]}...\\\")\\n\\n    env.close()\\n    print(\\\"\\\\n=== All tests completed ===\\\")\\n    \\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.35}}"

Use this when you are \\\"\\n        \\\"uncertain about the best next step.\\\\n\\\"\\n        \\\"   Format: &lt;VERIFY>&lt;think>[Assessment: positive/neutral/negative] - evaluate \\\"\\n        \\\"your progress.\\\\n\\\"\\n        \\\"   [Reasoning: analyze what has been accomplished and what remains.]\\\\n\\\"\\n        \\\"   [Suggestion: the best next action from admissible actions.]&lt;/think>\\\"\\n        \\\"&lt;answer>your action&lt;/answer>\\\\n\\\"\\n        \\\"\\\\n\\\"\\n        \\\"2. **Act directly**: Skip verification and directly output your action when \\\"\\n        \\\"you are confident. This saves computation.\\\\n\\\"\\n        \\\"   Format: &lt;think>brief reasoning&lt;/think>&lt;answer>your action&lt;/answer>\\\\n\\\"\\n        \\\"\\\\n\\\"\\n        \\\"Choose wisely: verification helps when you are unsure, but costs resources \\\"\\n        \\\"when the best action is obvious.\\\"\\n    )\\n\\n\\ndef build_biavr_user_prompt_suffix() -> str:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Additional instruction appended to each user turn for B-IAVR.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    return (\\n        \\\"Decide whether to verify (output &lt;VERIFY> first) or act directly. \\\"\\n        \\\"Your reasoning MUST be enclosed within &lt;think> &lt;/think> tags. \\\"\\n        \\\"Then choose an admissible action and present it within &lt;answer>...&lt;/answer> tags.\\\"\\n    )\\n\\n\\ndef build_no_guidance_prompt() -> str:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"System prompt for the counterfactual a_pre generation (no self-guidance).\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    return (\\n        \\\"You are an expert agent in the ALFRED Embodied Environment.\\\\n\\\"\\n        \\\"Complete household tasks by navigating and interacting with objects.\\\\n\\\"\\n        \\\"\\\\n\\\"\\n        \\\"Choose the best action from the admissible actions.\\\\n\\\"\\n        \\\"Your reasoning MUST be enclosed within &lt;think> &lt;/think> tags.\\\\n\\\"\\n        \\\"Then present your action within &lt;answer>...&lt;/answer> tags.\\\"\\n    )\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.36}}"}

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"B-IAVR reward computation and Lagrangian dual update.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\\nfrom typing import List\\n\\n\\ndef biavr_reward(\\n    verify_t: bool,\\n    action_changed_t: bool,\\n    lambda_cost: float,\\n    alpha: float = 1.0,\\n) -> float:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Compute per-step B-IAVR reward.\\n\\n    r_step(t) = -lambda * 1[verify_t] + alpha * 1[verify_t AND action_changed_t]\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if not verify_t:\\n        return 0.0\\n    r = -lambda_cost\\n    if action_changed_t:\\n        r += alpha\\n    return r\\n\\n\\ndef lagrangian_update(\\n    lambda_t: float,\\n    mean_verify_rate: float,\\n    beta: float = 0.3,\\n    eta: float = 0.01,\\n) -> float:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Lagrangian dual variable update.\\n\\n    lambda_{t+1} = max(0, lambda_t + eta * (mean_verify_rate - beta))\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    return max(0.0, lambda_t + eta * (mean_verify_rate - beta))\\n\\n\\n@dataclass\\nclass BIAVRTracker:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Tracks B-IAVR statistics across a training run.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    lambda_cost: float = 0.1\\n    alpha: float = 1.0\\n    beta: float = 0.3\\n    eta: float = 0.01\\n\\n    _verify_decisions: List[bool] = field(default_factory=list)\\n    _action_changed: List[bool] = field(default_factory=list)\\n    _step_rewards: List[float] = field(default_factory=list)\\n\\n    def record_step(self, verify: bool, action_changed: bool) -> float:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Record a step and return its B-IAVR reward.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        r = biavr_reward(verify, action_changed, self.lambda_cost, self.alpha)\\n        self._verify_decisions.append(verify)\\n        self._action_changed.append(action_changed)\\n        self._step_rewards.append(r)\\n        return r\\n\\n    @property\\n    def verify_rate(self) -> float:\\n        if not self._verify_decisions:\\n            return 0.0\\n        return sum(self._verify_decisions) / len(self._verify_decisions)\\n\\n    @property\\n    def informativeness_rate(self) -> float:\\n        verified = [(v, c) for v, c in zip(self._verify_decisions, self._action_changed) if v]\\n        if not verified:\\n            return 0.0\\n        return sum(c for _, c in verified) / len(verified)\\n\\n    @property\\n    def total_step_reward(self) -> float:\\n        return sum(self._step_rewards)\\n\\n    def update_lambda(self) -> float:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Update lambda based on current verify rate and reset counters.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        self.lambda_cost = lagrangian_update(\\n            self.lambda_cost, self.verify_rate, self.beta, self.eta\\n        )\\n        self._verify_decisions.clear()\\n        self._action_changed.clear()\\n        self._step_rewards.clear()\\n        return self.lambda_cost\\n\\n    def get_metrics(self) -> dict:\\n        return {\\n            \\\"biavr/lambda\\\": self.lambda_cost,\\n            \\\"biavr/verify_rate\\\": self.verify_rate,\\n            \\\"biavr/informativeness_rate\\\": self.informativeness_rate,\\n            \\\"biavr/total_step_reward\\\": self.total_step_reward,\\n            \\\"biavr/n_steps\\\": len(self._verify_decisions),\\n        }\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.36}}"}

(mean_verify_rate - beta))\\n\\n\\n@dataclass\\nclass BIAVRTracker:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Tracks B-IAVR statistics across a training run.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    lambda_cost: float = 0.1\\n    alpha: float = 1.0\\n    beta: float = 0.3\\n    eta: float = 0.01\\n\\n    _verify_decisions: List[bool] = field(default_factory=list)\\n    _action_changed: List[bool] = field(default_factory=list)\\n    _step_rewards: List[float] = field(default_factory=list)\\n\\n    def record_step(self, verify: bool, action_changed: bool) -> float:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Record a step and return its B-IAVR reward.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        r = biavr_reward(verify, action_changed, self.lambda_cost, self.alpha)\\n        self._verify_decisions.append(verify)\\n        self._action_changed.append(action_changed)\\n        self._step_rewards.append(r)\\n        return r\\n\\n    @property\\n    def verify_rate(self) -> float:\\n        if not self._verify_decisions:\\n            return 0.0\\n        return sum(self._verify_decisions) / len(self._verify_decisions)\\n\\n    @property\\n    def informativeness_rate(self) -> float:\\n        verified = [(v, c) for v, c in zip(self._verify_decisions, self._action_changed) if v]\\n        if not verified:\\n            return 0.0\\n        return sum(c for _, c in verified) / len(verified)\\n\\n    @property\\n    def total_step_reward(self) -> float:\\n        return sum(self._step_rewards)\\n\\n    def update_lambda(self) -> float:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Update lambda based on current verify rate and reset counters.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        self.lambda_cost = lagrangian_update(\\n            self.lambda_cost, self.verify_rate, self.beta, self.eta\\n        )\\n        self._verify_decisions.clear()\\n        self._action_changed.clear()\\n        self._step_rewards.clear()\\n        return self.lambda_cost\\n\\n    def get_metrics(self) -> dict:\\n        return {\\n            \\\"biavr/lambda\\\": self.lambda_cost,\\n            \\\"biavr/verify_rate\\\": self.verify_rate,\\n            \\\"biavr/informativeness_rate\\\": self.informativeness_rate,\\n            \\\"biavr/total_step_reward\\\": self.total_step_reward,\\n            \\\"biavr/n_steps\\\": len(self._verify_decisions),\\n        }\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": standard ALFWorld action formats:\\n      \\\"go to desk 1\\\"           -> (\\\"go\\\", \\\"desk 1\\\", None)\\n      \\\"take lamp 1 from desk 1\\\" -> (\\\"take\\\", \\\"lamp 1\\\", \\\"desk 1\\\")\\n      \\\"put lamp 1 in/on desk 1\\\" -> (\\\"put\\\", \\\"lamp 1\\\", \\\"desk 1\\\")\\n      \\\"open fridge 1\\\"          -> (\\\"open\\\", \\\"fridge 1\\\", None)\\n      \\\"close drawer 1\\\"        -> (\\\"close\\\", \\\"drawer 1\\\", None)\\n      \\\"use desklamp 1\\\"        -> (\\\"use\\\", \\\"desklamp 1\\\", None)\\n      \\\"heat apple 1 with microwave 1\\\" -> (\\\"heat\\\", \\\"apple 1\\\", \\\"microwave 1\\\")\\n      \\\"cool apple 1 with fridge 1\\\"    -> (\\\"cool\\\", \\\"apple 1\\\", \\\"fridge 1\\\")\\n      \\\"clean mug 1 with sinkbasin 1\\\"  -> (\\\"clean\\\", \\\"mug 1\\\", \\\"sinkbasin 1\\\")\\n      \\\"examine mirror 1\\\"      -> (\\\"examine\\\", \\\"mirror 1\\\", None)\\n      \\\"inventory\\\"              -> (\\\"inventory\\\", None, None)\\n      \\\"look\\\"                   -> (\\\"look\\\", None, None)\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    action_str = \\\" \\\".join(action_str.strip().lower().split())\\n\\n    if action_str in (r\\\"^(put)\\\\s+(.+?)\\\\s+(?:in|on)\\\\s+(.+)$\\\",),\\n        (r\\\"^(heat|cool|clean|slice)\\\\s+(.+?)\\\\s+with\\\\s+(.+)$\\\",),\\n    ]\\n    for (pattern,) in two_arg_patterns:\\n        m = re.match(pattern, action_str)\\n        if m:\\n            verb, obj, recep = m.group(1), m.group(2).strip(), m.group(3).strip()\\n            return (verb, obj, recep)\\n\\n    one_arg_patterns = [\\n        r\\\"^(go\\\\s+to)\\\\s+(.+)$\\\",\\n        r\\\"^(open|close|toggle|use|examine)\\\\s+(.+)$\\\",\\n    ]\\n    for pattern in one_arg_patterns:\\n        m = re.match(pattern, None)\\n\\n    parts = action_str.split(maxsplit=1)\\n    if len(parts) == 2:\\n        return (parts[0], parts[1], None)\\n    return (action_str, None, None)\\n\\n\\ndef actions_equivalent(action_a: str, action_b: str) -> bool:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Check if two ALFWorld actions are semantically equivalent.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    return canonicalize_action(action_a) == canonicalize_action(action_b)\\n\", action_str)\\n        if m:\\n            verb, obj = m.group(1), m.group(2).strip()\\n            if verb == \\\"go to\\\":\\n                verb = \\\"go\\\"\\n            return (verb, obj, None)\\n\\n    parts = action_str.split(maxsplit=1)\\n    if len(parts) == 2:\\n        return (parts[0], parts[1], None)\\n    return (action_str, None, None)\\n\\n\\ndef actions_equivalent(action_a: str, action_b: str) -> bool:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Check if two ALFWorld actions are semantically equivalent.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    return canonicalize_action(action_a) == canonicalize_action(action_b)\\n\",       "result": "{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nThis is the context manager for the LLM agent.\\nauthor: Kangrui Wang, Zihan Wang\\ndate: 2025-03-30\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nfrom itertools import zip_longest\\nimport logging\\n\\nimport torch\\nimport numpy as np\\nfrom typing import List, Dict, Any, Optional, Union, Tuple\\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\\nimport re\\nfrom verl import DataProto\\nfrom verl.utils.dataset.rl_dataset import collate_fn\\nfrom transformers import AutoTokenizer\\nimport hydra\\nfrom ragen.utils import register_resolvers\\nfrom ragen.env import REGISTERED_ENV_CONFIGS\\nfrom tensordict import TensorDict\\n\\nfrom dataclasses import asdict\\nfrom ragen.llm_agent.memory.factory import create_memory\\nfrom ragen.llm_agent.memory.base import BaseMemory\\nregister_resolvers()\\n\\ndef get_special_tokens(tokenizer: AutoTokenizer):\\n    if \\\"qwen\\\" in tokenizer.name_or_path.lower():\\n        special_token = tokenizer.encode(\\\"<|im_start|>\\\")[0]\\n        reward_token = tokenizer.encode(\\\"<|im_end|>\\\")[0]\\n    elif \\\"llama-3\\\" in tokenizer.name_or_path.lower():\\n        special_token = 128006\\n        reward_token = 128009\\n    else:\\n        raise ValueError(f\\\"Unsupported model: {tokenizer.name_or_path}\\\")\\n    return special_token, reward_token\\n\\ndef get_masks_and_scores(input_ids: torch.Tensor, tokenizer: AutoTokenizer, all_scores: List[List[float]] = None, use_turn_scores: bool = False, enable_response_mask: bool = False):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    input_ids: shape (bsz, seq_len)\\n    Get loss mask that only learns between <|im_start|>assistant and <|im_end|>. Currently only supports qwen.\\n    NOTE: important! This assumes that the input_ids starts with system and then user & assistant in alternative ways\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    special_token, reward_token = get_special_tokens(tokenizer)\\n    \\n    turn_starts = torch.where(input_ids == special_token, 1, 0)\\n    turn_indicators = torch.cumsum(turn_starts, dim=-1)\\n    if enable_response_mask:\\n        loss_mask = (turn_indicators % 2 == 1) & (turn_indicators > 1) # only learns all assistant turns\\n    else:\\n        loss_mask = (turn_indicators > 1) # learns everything after system prompt\\n    response_mask = (turn_indicators % 2 == 1) & (turn_indicators > 1)\\n    \\n    score_tensor = torch.zeros_like(input_ids, dtype=torch.float32)\\n    if use_turn_scores:\\n        for idx, scores in enumerate(zip_longest(*all_scores, fillvalue=0)):\\n            scores = torch.tensor(scores, dtype=torch.float32)\\n            turn_indicator = idx * 2 + 3 # 0: pad. 1: system. 2+2n: user. 3+2n: assistant\\n            reward_position = (input_ids == reward_token) & (turn_indicators == turn_indicator)\\n            # Set the last token of the rows where all positions are False to True\\n            reward_position[~reward_position.any(dim=-1), -1] = True\\n            score_tensor[reward_position] = scores\\n        if \\\"qwen\\\" in tokenizer.name_or_path.lower():\\n            # for Qwen, there is a \\\"\\\\n\\\" between special token and reward token, so we shift this to make sure reward is assigned to the last token of a turn\\n            score_tensor = score_tensor.roll(shifts=1, dims=-1)\\n    else:\\n        scores = [sum(i) for i in all_scores]\\n        score_tensor[:, -1] = torch.tensor(scores, dtype=torch.float32)\\n    score_tensor = score_tensor[:, 1:] #       "result": "{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"    def fit(self):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        The training loop of PPO.\\n        The driver process only need to call the compute functions of the worker group through RPC\\n         to construct the PPO dataflow.\\n        The light-weight advantage computation is done on the driver process.\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n        from omegaconf import OmegaConf\\n\\n        from verl.utils.tracking import Tracking\\n\\n        logger = Tracking(\\n            project_name=self.config.trainer.project_name,\\n            experiment_name=self.config.trainer.experiment_name,\\n            default_backend=self.config.trainer.logger,\\n            config=OmegaConf.to_container(self.config, resolve=True),\\n        )\\n\\n        def _finish_logger():\\n            if hasattr(logger, \\\"finish\\\"):\\n                logger.finish()\\n\\n        self.global_steps = 0\\n\\n        # load checkpoint before doing anything\\n        self._load_checkpoint()\\n\\n        # perform validation before training\\n        # currently, we only support validation using the reward_function.\\n        if self.val_reward_fn is not None and self.config.trainer.get(\\\"val_before_train\\\", True):\\n            val_metrics = self._validate()\\n            pprint(f\\\"Initial validation metrics: {val_metrics}\\\")\\n            logger.log(data=val_metrics, step=self.global_steps)\\n            if self.config.trainer.get(\\\"val_only\\\", False):\\n                _finish_logger()\\n                return\\n\\n        # we start from step 1\\n            self.global_steps += 1\\n        else:\\n            print(f\\\"[Gradient Analysis] Probing resumed checkpoint at step {self.global_steps}\\\")\\n        last_val_metrics = None\\n\\n        def _process_batch_for_logging(batch):\\n            inputs_raw = batch.batch[\\\"input_ids\\\"]\\n            inputs = [self.tokenizer.decode(input_ids, skip_special_tokens=True) for input_ids in inputs_raw]\\n            outputs = [\\\"\\\"] * len(inputs)\\n            scores = batch.batch[\\\"rm_scores\\\"].sum(-1).cpu().tolist()\\n\\n            # <details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

````text
Now I understand the generation prompt mechanism. Key observation from `_fit_single_turn_history_start_to_max_len`:

```python
if add_generation_prompt and not include_assistant:
    if self.config.agent_proxy.enable_think:
        text += "&lt;think>"
    else:
        text += "&lt;answer>"
```

And in `_build_infer_samples`, I need to see how this is applied. Let me also look at the _build_infer_multi_turn_messages to see the full picture.

From the code I can see:
1. For inference (add_generation_prompt=True), the prompt ends with `<|im_start|>assistant\n` (from chat template) + `&lt;think>` (appended by code)


2. The model generates: `reasoning&lt;/think>&lt;answer>action&lt;/answer>`
3. After decoding, the response is `reasoning&lt;/think>&lt;answer>action&lt;/answer>`
4. Code prepends `&lt;think>` → `&lt;think>reasoning&lt;/think>&lt;answer>action&lt;/answer>` 

For B-IAVR, the approach differs significantly. The prompt shouldn't end with `&lt;think>` since the model needs to decide between `&lt;VERIFY>` and direct action. The model then generates either `&lt;VERIFY>&lt;think>...` or `&lt;think>...`, and after decoding I get the full text. The code shouldn't prepend `&lt;think>` in this case since the model is choosing its own path.

Now I'm seeing the bigger picture—I need to handle B-IAVR separately in the `_build_infer_samples` function, or add conditional logic to account for the different behavior. Let me trace through how `_build_infer_samples` and `_build_infer_multi_turn_messages` are called from `get_lm_inputs` with `prepare_for_update=False`, and where the tokenization happens.
````

The `get_rollout_states` method then packages these histories into env_output dicts that include the full history, cumulative reward, and metrics.

Now I'm thinking through the integration strategy. The key is to intercept the `&lt;VERIFY>` token in `get_env_inputs()` before it gets wrapped with the normal `&lt;think>` tags, then route that verification request through a separate handler while keeping the rest of the flow intact. Now I'm deciding how to detect B-IAVR mode—I'll add a config flag rather than hardcoding environment tag checks, then check for a `biavr` section in the yaml config to determine if it's enabled. Time to implement these changes across the codebase, starting with the context manager. , update lambda. I need to add the new config file for the AlfWorld phase B setup and write integration tests for the B-IAVR module. Before I start implementing, let me reconsider the prompt format carefully. The model needs to output `&lt;VERIFY>` as the first token when doing verification, followed by `&lt;think>` tags for reasoning, then `&lt;answer>` for the action. In the direct-act case, it skips the verify tag but still includes the thinking and answer tags. The current pipeline needs to handle both formats correctly.

I realize the issue: the prompt shouldn't end with `&lt;think>` in B-IAVR mode since the model will generate that tag itself depending on whether it's verifying or acting directly. I need to modify the sample building logic to skip appending that tag, and then update the response decoding to handle both the verification format and the direct-act format properly.

The parsing logic should still work as-is since it uses regex search rather than match, so it'll find the `&lt;think>` tags regardless of whether `&lt;VERIFY>` precedes them. The main changes are in the sample construction and the input preparation—I need to detect B-IAVR mode and conditionally skip the tag, then check for the `&lt;VERIFY>` prefix when processing responses to extract the verification time. I'm considering a simpler approach where I track verify decisions in agent_proxy during rollout and use those to compute biavr rewards afterward, rather than trying to retroactively detect them from the raw response. For the a_pre computation, I'll need to generate extra responses at rollout time for the environments that chose to verify.

text state\\n            entry['state'] = next_state\\n        else: # multimodal state\\n            entry['state'] = \\\"&lt;images>\\\" * len(next_state)\\n            entry['images'] = next_state\\n        entry['actions_left'] = actions_left\\n        \\n\\n... the envs have pre-defined action lookup\\n            rev_action_lookup = {v.lower(): k for k, v in action_lookup.items()}\\n            actions = [action.lower() for action in actions]\\n            mapped_actions = [rev_action_lookup[action] for action in actions if action in rev_action_lookup]\\n        return mapped_actions\\n    \\n    def _handle_mm_state(self, state: Union[str, np.ndarray, list[np.ndarray]]):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Handle the state from the environment\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        if isinstance(state, str): #
